summaries have 13 attacks = 450 cells; pre-breakdown summaries
have 5 sections = 125 cells). Name-keyed is the primary
comparison (task_id + cell key, duplicate attack kinds indexed
by occurrence); position-keyed is listed only where both sides
have 475 cells, else it misaligns and is marked n/a.
